### 2026-08-14 04:25:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Extends Record Streak — The S&P/TSX Composite Index added 0.3% to close at 36,759, extending its record-breaking streak amid lower oil prices and soft US producer inflation. Oil prices fell on weakening demand prospects. Easing inflationary pressures in the US, following tame PPI data, supported financials and other credit-sensitive shares. RBC added 0.6% and CIBC gained 1.1%. Tech stocks posted gains, tracking a strong session for US chipmakers and hyperscalers. Shopify advanced 5.3%, Constellation Software gained 2.8%, and Celestica added 2.6%. Brookfield (+0.7%) reported higher second-quarter distributable earnings per share. CCL jumped 6.9% after posting 9.1% sales growth and 10.7% adjusted EPS growth. Pan American Silver slumped 9.8% after posting EPS below expectations. Hydro One shed 0.5% despite beating quarterly estimates. Metro lost 0.5% after reporting weaker fiscal third-quarter adjusted earnings.

### 2026-08-14 04:18:37  #MKT News

【Kushner Plans Israel Visit Next Week to Discuss Gaza, Axios Says】Axios, citing five people, reported Kushner plans to visit Israel next week to discuss Gaza — his first trip since January. The visit coincides with the White House and Trump’s peace committee pushing a next-phase Gaza plan focused on disarming Hamas and a possible Israeli withdrawal. The Netanyahu government is highly skeptical of the U.S. plan and the upcoming October election has heightened political sensitivity. A U.S. official said, "We are aligned on strategic goals but Israel has expressed some concerns; we want to talk to ensure coordination." The official added the visit date could change. Kushner may also meet leaders of other regional states involved in Gaza. The trip indicates the White House does not want the Israeli election to block the next phase; Netanyahu is reluctant to make concessions that could damage his political standing but also seeks to avoid a clash with Trump before the vote.

### 2026-08-14 04:10:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Gain on Softer PPI Data — US stock indices closed higher on Thursday after soft price data consolidated bets on a Federal Reserve rate hold next month. The S&P 500 rose 0.6% to a new record, while the Nasdaq jumped 1.1%, also nearing it's all-time closing high, and the Dow added 70 points. Headline and core producer prices were lower than expected in July, in line with yesterday’s tame CPI report and easing recent concerns over hawkish signals from FOMC members. Chip stocks extended gains from yesterday, tracking their South Korean counterparts. Reports that Anthropic is pursuing an IPO and seeking to acquire Decart AI for $6 billion continued to support the outlook for AI-related spending. Micron (+4.2%), Intel (+%), and Marvell (+3.6%) rose, while Sandisk surged 13.7%. In contrast, Cisco slumped 8.4% after projecting a slowdown in sales tied to AI data centers this fiscal year. Bank and industrial stocks also pulled back, with Bank of America (-1.1%) and RTX (-1%) falling, limiting gains for the Dow.

### 2026-08-14 03:46:55  #MKT News #WDAY.O #Impact bullish

【Talks with Silver Lake send Workday shares up nearly 30%】Workday shares jumped as much as ~30% intraday on Thursday, triggering multiple temporary trading halts, after reports that Silver Lake is in acquisition talks; sources say discussions have been ongoing. The rally pushed Workday’s market value above $50.0 bln. The stock had been under longer-term pressure on worries that AI will reshape the software sector; a successful buyout would signal renewed private-capital interest in software assets. Jefferies analyst Til said CEO Aneel Bhusri’s close ties to Silver Lake make a deal plausible. Workday beat estimates in its May quarter, raised guidance, and said AI trends are producing tangible benefits.

### 2026-08-14 03:12:16  #國際

【俄外長：將扣押西方國家船隻作為對等反制】俄羅斯外交部長拉夫羅夫13日對俄媒表示，俄方將“自行決定”扣押哪些西方國家船隻，作為對西方國家扣押俄船隻行為的反制。拉夫羅夫說，西方國家把“強盜行徑和海盜行為”當作官方政策，俄羅斯將從服務於西方國家利益的船隻中自主選擇扣押目標，至於如何處理“將由俄方自行決定”。西方國家指責俄羅斯利用油輪等商用船隻逃避西方制裁，將這些商用船隻稱為“影子艦隊”。歐洲多國近來在相關水域攔截多艘所謂俄“影子艦隊”船隻。拉夫羅夫表示，所有不遵從歐盟要求的船隻，無論懸掛何種旗幟，都被歐盟劃定為“影子艦隊”。歐盟這一“非法決定”意在遏制俄羅斯在全球市場獲利。這種做法“極其危險”。（新華社）

【US forms first multinational UAS task force to bolster Middle East strike capability】CENTCOM announced on Aug. 13 it has formed a multi-domain, multinational unmanned systems strike task force, Falcon Strike, to integrate unmanned combat capability across the Middle East. Falcon Strike will field air, surface and subsurface one-way attack unmanned systems operated jointly by US and regional partner forces. The unit follows CENTCOM’s first dedicated UAS strike task force, Scorpion Strike, stood up nine months earlier; Scorpion achieved milestones including the first ship-launched aerial attack UAS in December and deployments in Operation Epic Anger and July strikes on Iranian port facilities. CENTCOM commander Brad Cooper said Falcon Strike will build on Scorpion Strike’s experience to integrate new US and partner capabilities into a unified multi-domain, multinational deterrent.

### 2026-08-14 00:42:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Lumber Extends Decline Amid Tariff Uncertainty — Lumber prices fell below $570 per thousand board feet as tariff uncertainty continued to weigh on demand. Canada’s minister responsible for US trade relations said the country remains committed to negotiations, although reports indicated Canadian officials were dissatisfied with the latest US offer and continued to push for zero tariffs on softwood lumber. Earlier, the US imposed tariffs on several Canadian lumber-related products, while Canadian softwood lumber was excluded. Still, US demand for Canadian lumber fell 1.7% in the first half of the year, while its share of the US market declined slightly. Shipments to markets outside the US also fell 3%. Meanwhile, mortgage rates rose to their highest level in a year, according to Freddie Mac, while mortgage applications declined in the final weeks of July. Construction spending on lumber-intensive single-family housing fell 3.3% year-on-year in June, adding to demand concerns.

Meanwhile, exports rose 12.3% to €32 billion, supported by significant increases in sales of computer equipment, raw silver and refined copper. However, exports from the automotive and furniture industries declined.

### 2026-08-13 23:47:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

Chip Producers Return to Market Lead — US stocks remained mostly higher on Thursday after soft price data consolidated bets of a Federal Reserve rate hold next month. The S&P 500 rose 0.5% to a new record and the Nasdaq jumped 0.8%. Headline and core producer prices were lower than expected in July, aligned with the tame CPI report from yesterday and limiting recent concerns of hawkish signals by FOMC members. Chip stocks extended gains from yesterday, tracking counterparts in South Korea. Reports that Anthropic is chasing an IPO and is aiming to acquire Decart AI for $6 billion continued to underpin the outlook for compute expenditures. Micron, Intel, and Marvel rose more than 6%, while Sandisk surged 15%. In turn, Cisco slumped 8% after it projected a slowdown in sales tied to AI data centers for this fiscal year. Also, bank holdings and industrial companies pulled back with Bank of America and RTX each dropping 1.5%, pressuring the Dow to below the flatline.

【USS Washington to relieve USS Lincoln in Middle East; lawmakers cite crew fatigue】US officials told the Wall Street Journal the carrier USS Washington will rotate into the Middle East to relieve USS Lincoln under a planned swap. Lincoln has been on an extended deployment since November with few port calls and was redeployed to the region in January ahead of operations against Iran. The carrier and its air wing were central to the US 'Epic of Fury' bombing campaign and later participated in a blockade of Iranian ports. Lawmakers say the sustained high operational tempo and prolonged deployment have strained crew welfare and are pressing the Pentagon for more information on living conditions and mental-health issues.

### 2026-08-13 23:32:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Colombia Consumer Confidence Weakens in July — Colombia’s Consumer Confidence Index fell 3.6 percentage points to 20.7% in July 2026 from 24.3% in June. The decline was driven by a 7.4-point drop in the Economic Conditions Index to 6.4%, while the Consumer Expectations Index fell 1.1 points to 30.3%. The share of respondents expecting “good economic times” over the next 12 months declined to 24.8% from 31.3%. Meanwhile, expectations for an improvement in the national economy rose to 24.3% from 22.5%, while households reported a better outlook for their current financial situation, with the indicator rising to 41.8% from 40.2%. The share of respondents reporting a deterioration in their household’s financial condition fell slightly to 11.1% from 11.5%. Meanwhile, willingness to purchase durable goods slumped to 1.7% from 16.1%.

【Fitch: US private credit 12-month default rate hits record 6.1% in July】Fitch said its sample of 1,300 US private credit borrowers recorded a 12-month rolling default rate of 6.1% in July, up from 6.0% in June and the highest since Fitch began tracking the series in August 2024. For the 300 private issuers Fitch rates for insurers, the 12-month default rate fell to 8.6% in July from 9.4% in June. Fitch logged three private credit defaults in July — one new borrower and two repeat defaulters — and 83 borrowers triggered 105 default events in the 12 months to July 2026.

### 2026-08-13 23:03:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Holds Near 2-Month High — Platinum futures held near $1,730 per ounce after reaching a two-month high, as traders assessed the outlook for demand. Rising hybrid vehicle production has provided near-term support for autocatalyst consumption, although markets remain cautious about the broader shift toward electric vehicles, which recorded a fifth consecutive monthly increase in July. Meanwhile, uncertainty over shipments through the Strait of Hormuz kept industrial buyers cautious. On the supply side, concerns over power disruptions and maintenance bottlenecks at South African mines continued to underpin prices, with the platinum market expected to remain in deficit. Additional support came from plans by the world’s largest jeweler to replace some sterling-silver jewelry with platinum-plated alternatives. Meanwhile, AI and data-center expansion are emerging as potential new sources of demand, with South Africa’s Valterra Platinum estimating AI-related demand could grow fivefold by 2030.

### 2026-08-13 22:50:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Crude Oil Drops Below $87 — Brent crude oil fell below $87 a barrel on Thursday, after five consecutive sessions of gains, as investors shifted attention toward weakening demand prospects and continued disruption around the Strait of Hormuz. Traders are closely monitoring how much oil is moving through the key waterway, as diplomatic efforts to end the Iran war and restore normal shipping remain stalled. Meanwhile, major producers including Saudi Arabia and the UAE are seeking alternative ways to maintain exports, with some vessels reportedly switching off transponders while navigating the strait. The International Energy Agency lowered its global oil demand outlook, warning that prolonged conflict and elevated prices are increasingly weighing on consumption. The agency estimates the global market could face a supply deficit of 1.8 million barrels per day this quarter, more than twice its previous forecast. US crude inventories also surged by 17.4 million barrels last week.

【美聯儲巴爾金：勞動力市場並不像資料顯示的那樣強勁】美聯儲巴爾金表示，企業對企業銷售的公司對定價能力充滿信心；企業對消費者銷售的公司則不太確定能否轉嫁成本，美聯儲目前並未處於“前瞻性指引”狀態。他認為勞動力市場並不像資料顯示的那樣強勁，認為其較為脆弱，確實擔心低端消費者支出能維持多久。巴爾金表示，確實相信如果幫助人們理解反應函式，有助於市場發揮其作用，但也認為過於謹慎的指引會導致約束。巴爾金稱，企業通常不喜歡裁員，即使對招聘持謹慎態度，也更傾向於避免裁員。巴爾金表示，糾結於應關注通脹已逾五年高於目標，還是應關注其經歷兩輪跳升後正在放緩。整體通脹持續放緩的程度越高，就越有助於控制通脹預期。許多人工智慧應用案例似乎尚未成熟到足以導致員工人數大幅減少。人工智慧對就業的淨影響，不如其可能引發的就業崗位重新分配問題更值得關注。巴爾金表示，鑑於任何模型都存在標準誤差，很難判斷政策是否具有限制性。

### 2026-08-13 22:43:19  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

US Natgas Prices Fall by Over 2% — US natural gas prices fell more than 2% to around $2.73 per million British thermal units on Thursday, as strong production and ample inventories outweighed continued weather-driven demand. Energy companies added 36 billion cubic feet of gas to storage in the week ended August 7, above market expectations and the five-year average of 33 bcf. Production in the Lower 48 states averaged a record 111.2 bcfd in August, up from 110.7 bcfd in July, helping keep inventories above their five-year average since March. However, hotter-than-normal weather is expected to persist through August 28, supporting electricity demand for air conditioning and limiting the downside for gas prices. Meanwhile, around 490,000 homes and businesses remained without power after severe storms hit the US Midwest. Gas flows to the nine major US LNG export facilities eased to 17.1 bcfd in August, below July’s 17.2 bcfd and June’s monthly record of 17.4 bcfd.

### 2026-08-13 22:38:06  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Treasury Yields #US Economy #US10Y #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish #XAUUSD

【Goldman says Fed's July pause 'absolutely' correct; stay open until September】Goldman analyst Robert Kaplan said the Fed’s decision to hold in July was “absolutely” correct and urged policymakers to stay open-minded through September, arguing complex, offsetting inflation drivers make rigid forward guidance counterproductive. He cited inflationary pressure from AI build-out, tariffs, labor constraints and oil-price spikes, while AI applications are working the other way and accelerating disinflation. Kaplan said he could remain on hold if he sees meaningful improvement but wants to use the interval before September to reassess rather than lock in a view. He urged Fed speakers to use this month’s Jackson Hole symposium to briefly explain the July pause rather than give a purely “philosophical” speech. Kaplan said he is more worried about the long end of the US Treasury curve than the fed funds rate, attributing the global rise in long yields to a structural supply–demand imbalance driven by persistently loose fiscal deficits rather than Fed policy.

【Signs US inflation may have peaked; markets no longer fully pricing Fed hike this year】Signs US inflation have strengthened and bond traders are no longer fully pricing a Federal Reserve rate hike this year. A U.S. Treasury rally sent yields down as much as 9bp across maturities; the 30-year yield fell 8bp ahead of a new 30-year issue expected to price at the highest yield for that tenor since 2001. Benchmark oil dropped over 3% on Thursday — oil, the chief driver of UST moves since a late‑February US strike on Iran sparked a supply shock, is reinforcing views that US inflation has peaked. US data showed July PPI slowed and Wednesday’s CPI indicated inflation eased for a second consecutive month. Short-dated interest-rate contracts rallied, lowering implied policy rates and signalling traders are scaling back Fed hike bets.

Baltic Dry Index Down for 4th Day — The Baltic Exchange's dry bulk freight index, which monitors rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, retreated for a fourth session on Thursday, dropping about 3.2% to its lowest since August 3 at 2,844 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, slipped further, down about 5.2% to 4,469 points; and the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell by 1.7% to 2,262 points, marking the second session of decreases. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index went up by 0.6% to 1,613 points.

### 2026-08-13 22:27:15  #公司 #市場 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲的哈馬克稱三個領域可能影響金融穩定 包括美債市場的高槓杆】美國克利夫蘭聯儲銀行行長貝絲·哈馬克表示，她目前重點關注三個可能影響美國金融穩定的領域，包括美國國債市場的槓桿水平。哈馬克週四在俄亥俄州Dayton出席活動時表示：“我們有大量未償債務，而這些債務是用大量槓桿來購買的。”她說：“這些債務的買家實際上也是借錢來買債，因此從這個角度看，這可能會在金融體系中造成一些不穩定。”她還表示，從國會目前的情況來看，“我看不到財政紀律大幅迴歸的跡象”。哈馬克稱，私人信貸的增長也是另一個需要關注的領域。她表示，從某些方面看，這是一件好事，因為私人信貸擁有較為穩定的資本來源，不會像銀行那樣面臨存款擠兌式的贖回壓力。不過，她指出：“我們缺乏足夠的透明度，也不知道這些機構在發放部分貸款時採用的信貸標準是什麼。”她補充稱，這意味著這一領域可能存在更多高風險貸款。哈馬克表示，她關注的第三個方面是人工智慧的發展。她說：“短時間內有大量資本投入這一領域，這讓人聯想到上世紀90年代的電信基礎設施建設熱潮。我們是否正在進入泡沫？”她還表示：“我們看到這些超大規模科技公司開始透過借款為投資融資。如果這些投資最終沒有他們預期的那麼賺錢，是否可能出現回撤？”哈馬克總結稱，這三方面都是她正在關注的問題，以觀察它們可能如何影響金融體系。

Palladium Falls From 2-Month High — Palladium futures slipped below $1,330 per ounce after hovering near a two-month high, weighed by concerns over the long-term impact of growing electric vehicle demand. Although rising hybrid vehicle demand has provided near-term support for autocatalyst consumption, markets remain cautious amid the broader shift in demand toward EVs, which recorded a fifth consecutive monthly increase in July, as the palladium market could still record a global surplus. On the other hand, concerns over power supply disruptions and maintenance bottlenecks at South African mines, alongside uncertainty surrounding Russian exports, continued to underpin prices. Meanwhile, market expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep borrowing costs unchanged at the next meeting increased following weaker-than-expected jobs and inflation data, generally supporting non-yielding precious metals.

### 2026-08-13 22:20:32  #公司

【國家外匯管理局深圳市分局：辦理外匯業務不收取任何形式的“備案費”】8月13日，國家外匯管理局深圳市分局釋出關於警惕冒用國家外匯管理局深圳市分局名義收取費用的公告。近期，有不法分子假冒國家外匯管理局深圳市分局名義，以紙質檔案等形式向個人傳送偽造的“繳費通知”，要求按跨境資金總額的一定比例繳納所謂“備案費”。現就有關情況公告如下：一、國家外匯管理局深圳市分局從未以紙質檔案、郵件或電話等形式向任何企業、個人等主體傳送要求繳納“備案費”等通知。國家外匯管理局深圳市分局辦理外匯業務，不收取任何形式的“備案費”。二、所謂傳送要求繳納“備案費”等檔案、郵件或電話的行為涉嫌詐騙。請廣大企業和個人收到類似檔案、郵件或電話後提高警惕，認真核實，切勿輕信，妥善保護好自身資訊和財產安全。一旦發現違法犯罪線索或遭遇損失，請及時向公安機關報案反映情況，積極提供線索，配合公安機關案件偵查，盡力挽回損失。

### 2026-08-13 22:12:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Brazil Industrial Confidence Improves in August — Brazil’s Industrial Business Confidence Index rose 1.9 points to 46.3 in August 2026 from 44.4 in July, ending a two-month streak of consecutive declines. Despite the improvement, the index remained below the 50-point threshold, marking the 20th consecutive month of lack of confidence. The Current Conditions Index rose 1.1 points to 42.7 as negative assessments became less intense and widespread. The improvement reflected better assessments of Brazil’s current economic conditions, which rose 1.9 points to 36.6, and companies’ own conditions, which increased 0.6 point to 45.7. The Expectations Index rose 2.3 points to 48.1, recovering part of the 3.1-point decline recorded in July and indicating that expectations for the next six months remained negative, although pessimism eased. Expectations for the Brazilian economy rose 2.5 points to 39.7, while expectations for companies’ own businesses increased 2.2 points to 52.3, signaling a return to optimism.

### 2026-08-13 21:54:34  #公司 #國際

【印尼鎳礦商協會：將有選擇地發放2026年鎳礦石額外生產配額】據報道，印尼鎳礦商協會週四表示，印尼政府將僅針對原料庫存偏低的冶煉廠，有選擇地批准額外的鎳礦石生產配額。當前市場正等待今年配額調整方案的明確資訊。該協會在宣告中指出，總體鎳礦石生產配額將維持在約2.6億至2.7億噸，而2025年的配額為3.79億噸。今年1月至7月，國內鎳礦石消費量已達1.4296億噸，約佔年度配額的53%至55%。印尼政府為所有礦產設定年度生產配額。該協會建議，應允許礦商今年再額外生產3000萬噸鎳礦石作為戰略緩衝，使總量達到約3億噸。此次增產是為了滿足預計於2026年投產的新增冶煉產能需求。印尼現有約80家鎳冶煉廠，若全部滿負荷運轉，年消耗礦石量約為3.15億噸。該協會同時呼籲政府在制定生產配額時提高透明度。協會秘書長表示：“若無透明且基於規則的修訂框架，全球市場將持續對個別公司配額的傳聞或決策過度反應，8月初倫敦金屬交易所（LME）的價格波動已充分印證這一點。”印尼能源與礦產資源部未就增產數量立即回應，但重申政府旨在保障本國資源的可持續性並支撐價格。

### 2026-08-13 21:54:28  #MKT News #Important #Commodities #Industrial Metals

【Indonesian miners group: govt to selectively grant 2026 nickel ore quotas to smelters with low stocks】Indonesia’s nickel miners association said the government will selectively approve additional 2026 nickel ore production quotas only for smelters with low raw-material inventories, as markets await clarity on this year’s quota adjustments. The association expects overall 2026 quotas to be held at about 260–270 mln tonnes versus 379 mln tonnes in 2025. Domestic nickel ore consumption from Jan–Jul reached 142.96 mln tonnes, roughly 53–55% of the annual quota. The group recommended permitting an extra 30 mln tonnes this year as a strategic buffer to lift supply toward ~300 mln tonnes and meet smelter capacity expected to start in 2026; Indonesia’s ~80 smelters would consume about 315 mln tonnes a year at full load. The association urged greater transparency in quota-setting, warning that opaque revisions prompt market overreactions — citing early-August LME price volatility. The Energy and Mineral Resources Ministry did not immediately specify volumes, reiterating policy aims to safeguard resource sustainability and support prices.

Silver Holds Near Recent Highs — Silver fluctuated around $65 an ounce, near the highest since June 22, as softer US inflation data reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve will leave interest rates unchanged next month. US producer-price inflation eased more than expected in July, helped by lower energy and food costs, providing further evidence that price pressures are not broadly accelerating. However, renewed tensions in the Middle East remain a risk, as any disruption could push energy prices higher and revive inflationary pressures. Oil is heading for a weekly gain as traders continue to monitor efforts by the US and Iran to end the conflict and reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Beyond monetary policy and geopolitical risks, silver continued to benefit from robust industrial demand, particularly from expanding solar-panel manufacturing and electricity-grid investment. Chinese imports of silver-bearing ores jumped 62.5% year-on-year in June to 219,000 tonnes.

【報告顯示德國企業破產數量持續處於高位】德國聯邦經濟和能源部13日釋出報告說，德國企業破產數量目前仍處於高位，預計未來幾個月還將有大量企業申請破產。 報告援引德國哈雷經濟研究所企業破產趨勢資料說，2025年8月至2026年7月期間，德國企業破產數量為18478家，同比增長8.3%。 哈雷經濟研究所資料顯示，今年7月德國企業破產數量為1689家，同比增長7%，較2016年至2019年同期平均水平高75%。7月規模最大的10%破產企業涉及1.3萬個工作崗位，其中工業領域涉及近6000個崗位。 德國信用報告機構“信貸改革”公司經濟學家帕特里克-路德維希·漢奇當天表示，預計到今年年底德國企業破產數量還將保持上升態勢，且至少到2027年中期都難以明顯緩解。他此前表示，只有德國經濟恢復穩定增長，企業破產形勢才可能趨於穩定。(新華社)

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Edges Down — South Africa's 10-year government bond yield eased to around 8.58%, down from an over one-week high of 8.61% hit on August 12, as falling oil prices eased inflation concerns and reduced bets for monetary policy tightening. Meanwhile, expectations of a softer US rate path supported demand for emerging-market assets, including South African bonds. On the domestic economic front, South Africa’s inflation rate rose to 5.0% in June 2026 from 4.5% in May, remaining well above the South African Reserve Bank’s (SARB) 3% target. Despite the increase, the central bank unexpectedly kept the repo rate unchanged at 7.0%, saying the 25-basis-point hike in May was sufficient to keep inflation pressures contained.

### 2026-08-13 21:14:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Holds Pullback — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note eased to 4.66% on Thursday, pulling back further since testing 19-month highs of 4.75% two sessions prior after the release of soft producer price data. Producer prices were unchanged from the previous month in July, below expectations of a 0.2% increase, and aligning the tame signals from consumer inflation data the previous session. The data consolidated the view that current inflation levels do not warrant a rate hike by the Federal Reserve next month. Still, the drop in yields were more muted in the longer end of the curve as lingering risks on energy supply maintained inflationary risks in the near term. This was magnified by the trend of a weakening Japanese yen, risking selling pressure from the largest foreign holder of US Treasury notes should the country intervene in the foreign exchange market. Such concerns were reflected by a tail in the latest 10-year note auction.

### 2026-08-13 21:04:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

DXY After PPI Report — The dollar index fell to around 99.9 on Thursday after softer-than-expected US producer inflation eased concerns over renewed price pressures and strengthened expectations for a cautious Federal Reserve policy stance. The Producer Price Index was unchanged in July, missing forecasts for a 0.2% increase, while recent consumer price data also pointed to moderating inflation. As a result, traders increased bets that the Fed will leave interest rates unchanged at its September 15-16 meeting, with markets pricing around a 65% probability of a hold. The latest figures suggest that the initial inflationary impact of the Middle East conflict and higher energy costs may be fading. However, renewed tensions in the region continue to pose risks to the inflation outlook. Meanwhile, weekly jobless claims increased but remained historically low, although recent employment data indicated that the US labor market has been weaker than previously estimated.

【美聯儲巴爾金：通脹出現回落訊號，應當維持利率不變】里士滿聯儲行長湯姆・巴爾金表示，鑑於通脹出現回落訊號，應當維持利率不變，但也承認部分物價壓力存在固化風險，最終可能迫使政策制定者收緊貨幣政策。“當前高通脹很大一部分來自衝擊因素，這類衝擊終將消退。” 巴爾金週四在南卡羅來納州格林維爾發表講話，提及關稅以及伊朗戰爭帶來的石油衝擊。但他補充道，如果供應鏈困境與人工智慧投資熱潮延續，可能催生更持久的物價壓力。“通脹居高不下為時已久，企業與消費者的物價預期存在上行風險。” 他稱，“一旦出現這種情況，就需要政策出手，推動通脹完全回落至目標。”巴爾金並未透露 9 月議息會議上自己會傾向何種政策選項。美聯儲上月第五次維持利率不變。不過，越來越多官員主張，要實現 2% 通脹目標，需要實施更緊縮的貨幣政策。

US Core Producer Prices Rise Less than Expected — Core producer prices in the United States, which exclude food and energy goods, rose 0.2% from the previous month in July of 2026, slowing from the upwardly revised 0.4% increase in the previous month. ### 2026-08-13 20:32:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 3

US Producer Prices Flat in July — US producer prices were unchanged in July from June 2026, following a revised 0.1% decrease in the previous period and below forecasts of a 0.2% gain. A 0.2% rise in services and a 2.2% increase in construction prices offset by a 0.7% decline in goods. Services prices rose at a slower pace than in June (0.2% vs 0.5%), while portfolio management costs jumped 6.5%, contributing significantly to the increase. By contrast, transportation and warehousing services fell 1.8%. Goods prices declined for a second consecutive month (-0.7% vs -1.4%), largely due to a 3.1% drop in energy prices, including a 5.7% plunge in gasoline prices. Food prices also fell 0.9% (vs -0.5%), while prices for goods excluding food and energy edged up 0.1% (vs 0.2%). Year-on-year, producer prices increased 4.7%, well below 5.5% in June and forecasts of 4.9%. Meanwhile, core producer prices rose 0.2%, below forecasts of 0.3% and the annual core rate came in at 4.2%, in line with expectations.

【United Overseas Bank (UOB): Fed to hold through end-2026; two 25bp cuts in 2027】United Overseas Bank (UOB) analyst Alvin Liew said after July US CPI that market odds of a September Fed hike have fallen and UOB’s base case is that the Fed will pause policy through all of 2026. UOB expects two 25bp cuts in 2027, likely at end-Q2 and end-Q4, gradually lowering the federal funds target to about 3.25% by end-2027, which it still views as its terminal rate estimate. The remnant vortex remains active and is drifting east into the sea under a westerly trough. Once over water it could regain marine heat and moisture and potentially re-intensify; a northward track would bring heavy rain to Northeast China, with Jilin and Heilongjiang at risk on Aug. 17-18. China's central meteorological agency forecasts roughly two additional typhoons may form in the northwest Pacific and South China Sea over the next 10 days.

【Standard Chartered: stronger-than-expected UK GDP unlikely to change rate outlook】EUR/GBP ticked up as ECB hawkish expectations supported the euro. ONS: June GDP +0.3% MoM (consensus 0.0); Q2 GDP +0.4% q/q, in line with expectations but below Q1’s +0.6%; YoY GDP +1.2% (consensus 1.1, prior 0.9). Standard Chartered says Q2 outturn exceeds the BoE’s July projection of q/q +0.3% but is unlikely to drive policy. Analysts flag inflation data (so far holding up) and a still-weak labour market as more important for rate decisions. With policy already viewed as restrictive, Standard Chartered keeps its call that the BoE will hold rates this year. Traders are focused on next week’s UK CPI for fresh policy clues. At the last meeting most BoE policymakers said financial conditions had tightened since the Middle East war, providing "sufficient insurance" against inflation risk from higher energy prices.

【新聞聯播：我國加快推動新一代通訊網建設】據新聞聯播報道，今年以來，我國加快推動新一代通訊網建設，推動相關基礎設施升級與前沿技術創新，持續完善空天地海一體化資訊通訊佈局。重慶涪陵的這家化工企業是國家首批萬兆光網試點專案。企業透過萬兆光網結合AI演算法開發的智慧報警系統，能以毫秒級速率傳送預警，並實時回傳8K高畫質影片。目前，重慶已經在園區、工廠、小區等多個場景部署這樣的萬兆光網試點專案。作為新一代通訊網的重要組成部分，我國從2025年開展萬兆光網試點，目前已建設136個試點專案。不僅是萬兆光網，今年以來，5G基站全面向5G-A升級演進，支撐智慧網聯新能源汽車、具身智慧等新興產業連線需要，6G也在加快技術部署。日前，工業和資訊化部組織開展6G創新發展部省協同試點專項行動，計劃到2029年，形成一批自主創新的6G技術方案，為6G商用落地提供支撐。同時，衛星網際網路配套建設也在提速。國家星網低軌星座、千帆商業星座持續批次發射組網，手機直連衛星技術完成實測驗證。下一步，國家將加速相關技術試驗和標準體系構建，推動建設陸海空天一體化的全域通訊底座。

### 2026-08-13 20:12:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Current Account #Importance 1

Poland Current Account Gap Wider than Anticipated — Poland recorded a current account deficit of €2,216 million in June 2026, the largest since August 2025, widening from the revised €445 million in the same month a year ago. Figures came above anaysts' estimates of a €764 million shortfall. The goods deficit jumped to €1,469 million from €133 million in June 2025, with imports climbing by 16.9% and exports rising at a slower 12.3%. At the same time, the primary income gap rose to €4,444 million from €3,998 million; and the secondary income went up to €203 million from €153 million. On the other hand, the services surplus increased slightly to €3,900 million from €3,839 million.

### 2026-08-13 20:12:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 1

Brazil Retail Sales Rise in June — Brazil's retail sales increased 0.5% month-on-month in June 2026, following a 0.3% rise in May and beating forecasts for a 0.3% gain. Four sectors posted growth, led by other personal and household goods (2.4%), hypermarkets and supermarkets (1.1%), furniture and household appliances (0.4%), and pharmaceutical and perfumery products (0.2%). Four sectors declined, led by books, newspapers and stationery (-9.9%), textiles, clothing and footwear (-2.6%), fuels and lubricants (-1.7%), and office, IT and communication equipment (-1.3%). Sales increased in 19 of Brazil's 27 states, led by Amazonas (3.0%), Espírito Santo (2.6%) and Roraima (2.5%). In contrast, expanded retail sales fell 1.0% month-on-month after declining 0.3% in May.

【滴水湖論壇累計推介超40款國產RISC-V晶片，量產率超90%】以RISC-V為代表的開放指令集架構已成為AI時代萬物智算互聯的重要支撐。為推動國產RISC-V晶片向縱深發展，8月13日，第五屆滴水湖中國RISC-V產業論壇（簡稱“滴水湖論壇”）在臨港新片區舉辦，旨在促進國產RISC-V晶片快速產業化落地和應用創新。上海開放處理器產業創新中心理事長，芯原股份董事長、執行長、總裁戴偉民介紹，滴水湖論壇已成功舉辦四屆，每屆論壇均推介約10款代表中國先進IC設計水平、緊密結合市場與應用需求的國產RISC-V晶片新品。在論壇累計推介的40餘款產品中，整體量產率已超過90%，被廣泛應用於消費電子、智慧家居、可穿戴裝置、通訊、汽車、工業控制等多個領域。這一量產成果充分表明，國產RISC-V晶片正加速從技術創新走向規模化應用，並已在多個行業和應用領域實現落地。（上觀新聞）

### 2026-08-13 20:09:33  #國際

【颱風“白海豚”對農業生產影響幾何】連日來，颱風“白海豚”給我國中東部多地帶來持續性、大範圍強降水天氣。中央氣象臺預計，未來3天，黃淮西南部、江淮、江漢、江南北部和東部仍有大到暴雨，部分地區有大暴雨。當前正值秋收作物產量形成關鍵期，持續降水對農業生產帶來哪些影響？中央氣象臺首席氣象服務專家宋迎波介紹，受“白海豚”影響，8月8日以來，浙江、上海、江蘇中南部、安徽大部、湖北西北部、河南等地出現較強風雨天氣。部分低窪農田出現積水，導致作物根系缺氧，易引發早衰，對秋收作物正常生長造成不利影響。同時，大風導致玉米等高稈作物倒伏，經濟林果出現折枝落果，溫室大棚和水產養殖設施也不同程度受損。“持續降雨容易使低窪農田發生漬澇災害，不利於正處在開花期的夏玉米和一季稻授粉，並可能導致棉花落鈴、爛鈴增多。伴隨降水而來的大風，容易引發植株倒伏，同時可能損毀溫室大棚、造成雨水倒灌，直接影響果蔬生產。”宋迎波說，高溫高溼環境也容易使農作物病蟲害蔓延。如何科學應對持續降雨形成的農業氣象災害？宋迎波建議，降水偏多地區及時疏通溝渠，確保排水暢通；雨後儘快排澇散墒，降低土壤溼度，恢復土壤通氣性，減少根系缺氧時間；受強降水沖刷出現脫肥的地塊應適時追施速效肥，促進作物恢復生長。澇漬過後，容易出現病蟲害，需抓住晴好天氣噴藥防治。宋迎波同時建議，對倒伏但莖稈未折斷的玉米，最好在倒伏後三至四天內扶正培土；棉田應及時整枝打杈，改善田間通風透光條件，減少花鈴脫落；果園方面，及時清理落果和斷枝。此外，各地要科學蓄水，為秋收作物後期用水做好儲備。國家氣象中心相關負責人表示，面對複雜的天氣形勢，國家氣象中心將聚焦秋糧生產關鍵時段，重點圍繞秋糧生長發育、產量形成及秋收秋種全鏈條開展精細化服務。9月將全面啟動秋收秋種氣象服務，推動定製化氣象資訊接入全國農機作業指揮平臺，靶向釋出農業氣象災害風險預警。同時，加強與農業農村、交通運輸、水利、糧食物資等部門會商研判，健全資訊共享與聯防聯控機制，全力保障秋糧穩產增收。（新華社）

---

【北京專設全國首例“老字號發展處” 出臺18條硬核舉措】近日，北京市商務局等6部門聯合印發了《北京市促進餐飲和食品領域老字號傳承發展若干措施》，北京市商務局相關處室加掛“老字號發展處”牌子，這也是目前全國商務系統中的首例。此次出臺的《若干措施》包括18條舉措，圍繞老字號保護、傳承、創新、發展和管理服務展開，推動傳統老品牌守正創新、提質煥新。北京市圍繞餐飲和食品類老字號人才、技藝、商標、史料、博物館建設等關鍵環節，持續加大政策扶持力度。透過緊扣消費升級趨勢與市場結構變化，北京正引導老字號企業主動適配現代消費場景，聚焦產品研發、品牌聯名、場景融合、業態創新等領域深耕突破。此外，北京市商務部門聯合文旅、市場監管、智慧財產權等多個部門，提出推進老字號傳統技藝活化傳承、梳理保護老字號文脈史料等專項舉措，切實築牢老字號傳承發展的核心根基。下一步，北京市相關部門將統籌用好各類宣傳平臺與優質資源，深入挖掘、集中展示老字號創新發展的生動實踐與典型案例，講好老字號匠心傳承、創新發展故事，推動更多老字號在傳承中創新、在創新中發展，持續煥發時代新活力。（央視新聞）

【俄副外長：美方未向俄方轉達烏方終戰新提議】俄羅斯外交部副部長里亞布科夫13日說，俄方不清楚烏克蘭總統澤連斯基就結束俄烏衝突向美方提出的相關建議，美方沒有透過外交渠道向俄方轉達任何資訊。澤連斯基日前表示，烏方已就一份旨在結束俄烏衝突的方案向美方提交一些新提議。里亞布科夫13日被媒體記者問及美方是否向俄方轉達相關提議時，以“沒有”作答。此外，針對美國總統特朗普的特使威特科夫、女婿庫什納訪俄一事，里亞布科夫說，在俄方更清楚地瞭解他們的實際計劃後，將迅速安排有關訪問。據俄羅斯媒體此前披露，威特科夫和庫什納將於近日訪問俄羅斯和烏克蘭。里亞布科夫曾於7月6日表示，儘管美國越來越背離俄美在烏克蘭問題上達成的協議，但俄方不會中斷同美方在該問題上的接觸，威特科夫和庫什納訪俄計劃已提上日程。美國和以色列今年2月底對伊朗發起大規模軍事行動以來，俄烏談判基本陷入停滯。威特科夫最近一次訪問俄羅斯還是在今年1月。據烏克蘭《基輔郵報》報道，特朗普開始第二個任期以來，威特科夫作為總統特使訪俄至少8次，庫什納參與其中一次。兩人在美國邁阿密等地與烏方特使有過多次會談，也曾與澤連斯基見面，但未訪問過烏克蘭。（新華社）

### 2026-08-13 19:54:31  #公司

【蒿慧傑葉牛平會見上海證券交易所黨委書記、理事長邱勇一行】8月13日，陝西省委常委、西安市委書記蒿慧傑，市長葉牛平會見上海證券交易所黨委書記、理事長邱勇一行。蒿慧傑、葉牛平代表市委市政府對邱勇一行表示歡迎。他們說，上交所是我國資本市場的重要陣地，長期以來在助推西安科技創新、企業成長、產業發展等方面發揮了重要作用，市委市政府對此表示衷心感謝。當前，西安正貫通落實習近平總書記歷次來陝考察重要講話重要指示，錨定高質量發展首要任務，一手抓老問題解決、一手抓新動能培育，加快推動“十五五”規劃落地見效，奮力譜寫中國式現代化西安實踐新篇章，這些都離不開資本市場的有力支撐。希望雙方縱深推進戰略合作，完善常態化對接機制，共同促進西安培育和利用多層次資本市場。一是充分發揮上交所平臺優勢，在上市輔導、政策諮詢、專業培訓、發行基礎設施REITs等方面給予西安企業更多指導幫助。二是常態化推進專案對接，推動更多西安企業到上交所發行債券，特別是用好用足科創債“活水”，不斷拓寬直接融資渠道。三是協同發揮好科創板作用，支援西安硬科技企業完善中國特色現代企業制度，促進研發生產能力與市場拓展能力穩步提升。四是密切協同配合，引導西安上市公司規範運作、誠信經營，打造資本市場“西安品牌”。五是加強人才交流與培訓合作，助力西安培養更多資本市場專業人才。（西安釋出）

【英國央行料AI泡沫破裂恐使資金同時撤出美股美債 對英國產生連帶衝擊】英國央行的分析顯示，如果人工智慧股票泡沫破裂，衝擊可能蔓延至英國，波及股價、英國國債收益率和企業信用市場。英國央行工作人員在一篇部落格文章中表示，如果美國大型科技公司的盈利令人失望，投資者可能會將其視為美國未來生產率前景被下調，並因此撤出美國資產，而不是向美國資產尋求避險。這可能導致美元走弱，並削弱過去在金融市場承壓時對英國等經濟體形成緩衝的一項因素。英國央行全球分析部門的Daniel Ostry、Roger Vicquéry和Emilio Zaratiegui寫道：“如果人工智慧推動生產率提升的預期落空，投資者可能同時撤出美國債券和股票市場。”他們表示，“這將與2008年全球金融危機等典型壓力情景形成鮮明反差”，當時，投資者尋求避險資產推動美元走強。這對英國起到了一定支援作用，一方面提升了英國出口競爭力，另一方面也提高了美元計價持倉的英鎊價值。

Uganda Leaves Key Policy Rate at 9.75% — The Bank of Uganda kept its benchmark interest rate steady at 9.75% during its August 2026 meeting, marking the eighth consecutive hold. Policymakers said inflationary pressures remained relatively contained despite higher oil prices. Annual inflation rose to 4% in July, the highest since September 2025, from 3.7% in June, but stayed below the central bank’s 5% medium-term target. Meanwhile, the economy remained resilient, with growth expected to accelerate to 7%-7.5% in FY2026/27, which began in July, from an estimated 6.4% in FY2025/26.

Sensex Finishes Slightly Higher — India’s BSE Sensex managed to close about 0.2% up at 78,080 after a choppy session on Thursday, reversing a two-day decline, helped by falling oil prices and an upbeat earnings season. The market also drew support from easing expectations of near-term Fed rate hikes, while lingering uncertainty in the Middle East kept gains in check. Buying in select heavyweight stocks and broader market segments helped offset weakness in financial, metal and energy stocks. Among individual stocks, IndiGo was the top gainer, rising 2.9%, followed by NTPC (2%), BEL (2%) and L&T (2%). At the same time, Tata Group shares saw some stabilization after a heavy sellof on Wednesday following the surprise N.Chandrasekaran's decision to resign. On the losing side, ICICI Bank declined 1.3%, making it the biggest laggard.Titan shed 1.1%%, while Reliance Industries slipped 0.8%. UltraTech Cement fell 0.7% following a block transaction involving 2.51 million shares, representing 0.86% of its equity.

### 2026-08-13 19:02:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Serbia Holds Rate Unchanged — The National Bank of Serbia held its key policy rate unchanged at 5.75% after its August 2026 meeting, holding benchmark borrowing costs at that level since September of 2024. The prolonged period of unchanged rates indicated the NBS sees risks of inflation and unemployment at a relatively balanced, despite macroeconomic headwinds from the war in the Middle East and its impact on global energy prices. July data showed that headline inflation fell to a five-year low of 1.9%, within the lower end of the tolerance band of 1.5% to 4.5%. Expectations of incoming base effects from the central bank drove the Executive Board to signal that inflation could rise to 4% by September. Similarly, the dinar held close to the 101 per USD mark, not too far from its strongest since late 2021. Still, expectations that the GDP will pick up during the year limited the upside of looser financial conditions.

### 2026-08-13 18:54:31  #公司 #市場 #國際

【美議員要求調查特朗普獲贈鑽戒】據美國媒體近日報道，美國民主黨籍聯邦參議員正要求比利時方面就贈予美國總統特朗普的一枚鑽戒作出說明。這枚鑽戒鑲嵌了321顆鑽石、重18克拉，主體材質為黃金。這枚戒指由比利時安特衛普世界鑽石中心（比利時鑽石行業的官方代表機構）主席於6月28日贈予美國駐比利時大使比爾·懷特，“旨在向特朗普致敬，以紀念美國建國250週年”。而幾周後，美國便宣佈對歐洲鑽石產業豁免新一輪關稅。 報道稱，在致安特衛普世界鑽石中心及製作這枚戒指的比利時高階珠寶商的信函中，美國民主黨籍聯邦參議員伊麗莎白·沃倫、理查德·布盧門撒爾警告稱，這一贈禮引發了嚴重的法律問題，並給人造成一種印象，即“外國企業、特殊利益集團只需用閃亮的禮物‘轟炸’特朗普，就能換取關稅豁免”。他們要求對方在8月24日前回應質詢，包括戒指的具體價值，以及在豁免政策公佈前是否與特朗普政府就關稅問題進行過任何磋商。 白宮方面否認向特殊利益集團輸送好處，稱豁免關稅的決定只是維護了特朗普政府此前根據美歐貿易協議為鑽石提供優惠待遇的承諾。有美國政府官員表示，這枚鑽戒屬於聯邦財產，如果特朗普有意保留，必須按市場價格向政府付款。據報道，這枚手工戒指的估值在2.5萬至3.5萬美元之間。（CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-08-13 18:51:15  #其他

【畝產突破400公斤 海南崖州瓜爾豆實現高產突破】近日，在三亞崖州區三更村瓜爾豆示範基地，幹豆畝產突破400公斤，產能達到國際先進水平，標誌著我國瓜爾豆本土化種植實現關鍵性突破。當前，三更村百畝瓜爾豆示範田進入豐收期。今年，科研團隊最新選育的新品種“明安1號灰A1”在當地推廣種植，依託成熟的本土化栽培技術，田間豐產效果亮眼。據瞭解，瓜爾豆是能源、食品、紡織、醫藥等領域的重要工業原料，尤其在油氣開採中應用廣泛、不可替代。僅中國石油每年瓜爾豆原料需求便達3萬噸，國內市場常年存在巨大原料缺口。長期以來，我國高階瓜爾豆膠原料高度依賴進口，產業鏈供應鏈存在明顯短板。為打破海外壟斷，育種專家黃明安退休後深耕海南田間二十餘年，持續攻關瓜爾豆本土化育種，並在2012年選育出國內首個審定的瓜爾豆品種“匯錦1號”，填補國產品種空白。（央視新聞）

【烏方稱扎波羅熱核電站今年已斷電14次】當地時間13日，烏克蘭國家核能發電公司釋出通報稱，扎波羅熱核電站裝置工況持續惡化，斷電事故頻發，核與輻射安全風險持續上升。該公司負責人科夫託紐克介紹，自俄烏衝突升級以來，扎波羅熱核電站已發生26次全站斷電，其中2026年前8個月便出現14次。核電站每次喪失外部供電後，都必須啟動備用電源，頻繁的電源切換操作進一步加劇了安全風險。科夫託紐克還表示，核電站裝置老化退化問題突出，柴油發電機組因頻繁啟停執行，裝置負荷大幅增加、磨損速度加快，執行穩定性顯著下降。扎波羅熱核電站是歐洲最大核電站之一。2022年2月烏克蘭危機全面升級後，俄方控制了扎波羅熱核電站。此後，該核電站多次遭到炮擊或無人機攻擊，引發國際社會對其安全的擔憂。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-13 18:23:33  #其他

【拓竹科技將入局消費級鐳射加工賽道 】，3D列印領域的頭部玩家拓竹科技，將正式切入一條新的賽道—消費級鐳射加工賽道。關於此次跨界，知情人士表示，“‘穀子經濟’、潮玩娃衣、個性化周邊定製等消費趨勢快速興起，消費級鐳射裝置正從小眾工具向大眾市場延伸，逐步成為個人創意落地和小微商業定製的實用型製造裝置。”事實上，拓竹的佈局已有先兆。2025年初，該品牌釋出了旗艦機型H2D——一款集3D列印、鐳射雕刻切割、數控刀切和繪圖於一體的“4-in-1”全能裝置，已顯露向鐳射加工領域拓展的意圖。如今正式獨立入局，背後是一個持續擴張的市場。行業公開資料顯示，2025年中國鐳射切割及雕刻機市場規模已達54.88億元人民幣，受益於製造業升級、文創產業爆發和消費DIY需求增長的三重驅動，國內行業複合年均增速為6.5%，高於全球平均水平。更細分的消費級鐳射雕刻機賽道增速更為突出：2024年全球市場規模約8億美元，出貨量約87.29萬臺；2025年已超12億美元，預計2026年將躍升至15億至18億美元。（藍鯨新聞）

Crude Oil Pulls Back on Thursday — Crude oil fell below $82 a barrel on Thursday, after five consecutive sessions of gains, as investors shifted their focus toward weakening demand prospects and continued disruption around the Strait of Hormuz. The International Energy Agency lowered its global oil demand outlook, warning that the prolonged Middle East conflict and higher prices are increasingly weighing on consumption. The agency estimates the global oil market could face a supply shortfall of 1.8 million barrels a day this quarter, more than twice its previous forecast, while supply remained 6.3 million barrels a day below year-earlier levels in July. Meanwhile, US crude inventories surged by 17.4 million barrels last week, their largest weekly increase since January 2023. Diplomatic efforts between the US and Iran to end the conflict and reopen Hormuz have made little progress, with attacks on shipping continuing and rhetoric intensifying. Despite Thursday’s decline, oil remained nearly 5% higher for the week.

### 2026-08-13 18:17:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Israel #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Israel Trade Deficit Narrows in July — Israel’s trade deficit narrowed slightly to USD 4.1 billion in July 2026, from USD 4.2 billion in the same month a year earlier. Exports rose 10.2% year-on-year to USD 5.62 billion, supported by stronger shipments of manufactured, mining and quarrying products excluding diamonds (10.2%), as well as diamond exports (49.9%). Meanwhile, imports increased 4.2% to USD 9.72 billion, driven by higher purchases of fuels (47.8%) and raw materials (4.5%), while consumer goods (0.5%) and investment goods (0.4%) recorded more modest gains. In the first seven months of 2026, the trade deficit widened 26.3% to USD 28.2 billion, from USD 22.3 billion in the same period of 2025.

### 2026-08-13 18:16:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Ireland Inflation Rate Steady at 3.4% — The annual inflation rate in Ireland rose by 3.4% in July 2026, matching the pace in the previous month. Prices increased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.7% vs 0.6% in June), clothing and footwear (8.5% vs 7.0%), housing and utilities (7.7% vs 7.3%), health (1.8% vs 1.7%), recreation and culture (0.5% vs 0.1%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.6% vs 1.9%). At the same time, price growth remained steady for education (at 8.9%), restaurants and hotels (at 3.6%), and information and communication (at 1.4%), while costs rebounded for furnishings and household equipment (0.1% vs -0.1%). Meanwhile, inflation grew at a softer pace for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.5% vs 2.6%), transportation (2.1% vs 4.1%), and insurance and financial services (5.5% vs 5.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.1% in July, easing from a 0.3% growth in the preceding period.

UK 10-Year Gilt Yield Edges Down — The UK 10-year gilt yield fell to around 4.97% as investors assessed stronger-than-expected economic data, interest-rate prospects and ongoing Middle East uncertainty. However, household consumption growth slowed to 0.2% in Q2. On the monetary policy front, the Bank of England left rates unchanged in July, with Governor Andrew Bailey saying the disinflation process remained on track despite ongoing external risks. In the US, recent economic data reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve may avoid raising rates next month. Meanwhile, uncertainty surrounding the US-Iran conflict and the Strait of Hormuz continued, with little progress reported in efforts to reopen the key shipping route.

【迅雷釋出二季度財報：總營收1.027億美元 同比增長38.9%】迅雷今天公佈了截至2026年6月30日第二季度未經審計的財報。公司總營收1.027億美元，同比增長38.9%。由於會員及廣告業務的提升，毛利潤同比增長23.1%，為5,730萬美元，毛利率為55.8%。 迅雷董事長兼執行長李金波表示：“公司本季度延續收入增長勢頭，這印證了To-C商業模式的正確性。期內總收入達1.027億美元，同比增長38.9%，毛利潤同比增長23.1%，得益於業務結構的持續最佳化與運營效率的有效提升。”資料顯示，迅雷會員服務營收4,450萬美元，同比增長22.6%，收入增長主要是由於服務需求增長。同時，在產品方面，迅雷上線了全新版本，並已在官網同步更新。直播和其他服務營收5,820萬美元，同比增長54.8%，得益於海外語音直播業務及廣告業務收入增加。本季度，迅雷加大了海外市場投入。據財報，直播業務成本增加致主營業務成本上漲，至4,480萬美元，去年同期為2,700萬美元。迅雷本季度在研發方面保持較高投入。研發費用2,140萬美元，佔總營收20.8%，而去年同期為1,660萬美元。（新浪科技）

### 2026-08-13 18:06:04  #MKT News #EURUSD #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact bearish

【Nomura: June hike likely the start; ECB may follow in September】An 83% majority of 69 economists polled expect the ECB to raise the deposit facility rate by 25bps to 2.50% in September; 80% expect the rate to remain at 2.50% through year‑end and 63% expect it to stay at or above 2.50% until Q3 2027. The share anticipating a September hike rose to 83% from 72% before July’s meeting and 65% in June, reinforcing consensus that June’s increase could be followed by another move. Nomura says sustained higher oil prices raise the risk of second‑round inflation effects; while the ECB can wait to see those effects it can also act preemptively, and a single small hike would look like a mere tweak—making a follow‑up move likely. Given June’s obvious choice, Nomura judges another rate rise highly likely.

### 2026-08-13 17:41:52  #宏觀

【全國市場監管系統查辦食品新增劑濫用】國家市場監督管理總局13日通報，自去年4月國務院食安辦等六部門聯合部署開展食品新增劑濫用問題綜合治理以來，各地市場監管部門聚焦重點食品品類和關鍵生產加工環節，整治食品新增劑濫用違法行為，綜合治理工作取得顯著成效。 各地市場監管部門以乳製品、肉製品、水產製品、澱粉及澱粉製品、食用植物油、飲料、複合調味料為重點，聚焦食品生產和餐飲加工過程中食品新增劑超範圍、超限量使用突出問題，加大監督抽檢力度，嚴厲查處防腐劑、甜味劑、著色劑濫用等突出違法行為。全國共查辦超範圍、超限量使用食品新增劑案件23603件，涉案總金額4362萬元，罰沒金額1.45億元，其中278件移送公安機關依法追究刑事責任，對食品新增劑濫用等違法行為形成強大震懾。 下一步，市場監管部門將結合監管中發現的突出問題，持續加大監督執法力度，聚焦奶茶、肉類熟食、糕點、麵食、酒類等重點品種深入推進綜合治理，持續推動企業落實食品安全主體責任，規範食品新增劑使用行為，依法查處重點案件、曝光典型案例，確保綜合治理取得實效。(新華社)

### 2026-08-13 17:41:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Mining Production #Importance 1

South Africa Mining Output Shrinks for 2nd Month — Mining production in South Africa fell by 4.4% year-on-year in June 2026, following a downwardly revised 5.1% drop in May. The largest negative contributors were PGMs, down 8.4% and weighing 2.4 percentage points, followed by coal (-6.6%; -1.7 points) and iron ore (-10.2%; -1.5 points). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, mining production rose by 0.3%, rebounding from a 5.2% decline in the preceding month. Mining activity shrank by 2.7% in the second quarter of 2026 compared with the first quarter of 2026.

【India July trade deficit widens as imports surge】India's July trade deficit widened to $31.98 bln from $30.4 bln in June, above a median economist estimate of $30.5 bln, as imports outpaced exports. Exports rose 19.6% YoY to $44.24 bln while imports increased 17.5% YoY to $76.22 bln. Strong import growth signals resilient domestic demand ahead of the festival season: vehicle sales are at record levels, credit growth hit a two-year high and GST receipts are rising at double-digit rates. Commerce Ministry secretary Rajesh Agrawal said export gains were led by oil products, electronics, engineering goods, gems and jewellery, iron ore, meat, dairy and poultry and seafood, with strong performance in Spain, Australia, Kenya and ASEAN markets.

### 2026-08-13 17:24:52  #宏觀

【我國正抓緊制定新一輪空氣質量持續改善行動方案】生態環境部副部長徐必久表示，我國正在抓緊制定新一輪空氣質量持續改善行動方案，也是第四個“大氣十條”。他是在13日國務院新聞辦公室舉行的“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會上介紹這一情況的。徐必久介紹，黨的十八大以來，我國先後印發實施三個“大氣十條”，分別是2013年的《大氣汙染防治行動計劃》、2018年的《打贏藍天保衛戰三年行動計劃》、2023年的《空氣質量持續改善行動計劃》，推動我國空氣質量明顯改善。資料顯示，2025年全國PM2.5濃度降到28微克/立方米，與2013年相比下降59%，重汙染天數比例降到0.9%，是有監測以來的最好水平。他說，當前，我國大氣汙染治理成效不充分、不穩固、不平衡的問題依然突出，空氣質量改善仍然處於從“明顯改善”向“全面改善”過渡的關鍵期。第四個“大氣十條”將重點在“更高、更準、更科學”上下功夫。（新華社）

【農業農村部組織專業力量指導颱風災後恢復生產】記者13日從農業農村部獲悉，近日，農業農村部組織專業力量，派出6個工作組和11個科技小分隊赴浙江等受颱風影響較重地區，指導災後恢復生產工作。種植業方面，農業農村部調配排澇救災機具，指導相關省份疏浚560萬畝農田溝渠，及時搶排田間積水，修復受損設施，減輕漬澇影響；推動落實秋糧抗逆防災噴施作業，促進作物恢復生長。畜牧業方面，印發洪澇災區動物防疫技術指南，指導地方開展死亡畜禽無害化處理和消毒滅源，防範次生疫情。漁業方面，統籌協調東海區上海、江蘇、浙江、福建4省（市）調整捕撈漁船出海時間，避開風雨影響；組織近4萬名船東船長線上接受漁船安全課程培訓，提升安全生產技能、壓實安全生產責任。（新華社）

【SpaceX安然度過華爾街頗為忌憚的首輪解禁 股價短短五日上漲35%】自SpaceX於6月上市以來，華爾街一直對8月6日心存擔憂。當天，限制早期投資者出售股票的首個鎖定期到期，大量股票勢將湧入市場。事實證明，這種擔憂並無必要。自解禁期結束以來，SpaceX股價一路飆升，短短五個交易日上漲35%，市值增加約5000億美元，並重新升破135美元的首發價。這波漲勢與市場此前對拋售潮即將來臨的擔憂形成反差，也讓投資者更樂觀地認為，未來幾輪解禁帶來的衝擊可能不會像之前擔心的那麼嚴重。持有SpaceX股票的Loxahatchee Capital管理合夥人兼投資委員會負責人Andrew Plum表示：“我們已經跨過了重大障礙，也就是未知本身。市場往往會提前反映一個負面事件，甚至可能反映過度；等事情真正發生時，結果通常沒有此前定價或預期的那麼糟。”

### 2026-08-13 17:12:37  #公司 #國際

【報告稱長江儲存快閃記憶體出貨量超過鎧俠 但銷售額仍排名第五】市場研究機構Counterpoint Research的資料顯示，按全球NAND快閃記憶體位元出貨量計算，長江儲存已經追上備受投資者青睞的鎧俠，同時進一步縮小了與三星電子和SK海力士之間的差距。鎧俠目前將業務重點放在面向美國資料中心運營商的高階儲存產品上。Counterpoint分析師MS Hwang和Neil Shah在報告中表示，鎧俠出貨量排名下降，部分原因在於伺服器產品價格較高。不過，如果按照銷售額而不是出貨量計算，長江儲存仍落後於美光科技和鎧俠，排名全球第五。分析師指出，長江儲存雖然已經具備相當大的出貨規模，但“規模尚未轉化為營收”。隨著人工智慧應用快速普及，企業對於低功耗、高能效資料儲存的需求正在上升，尤其是固態硬碟(SSD)。第二季度，SSD已佔全部NAND快閃記憶體出貨量的接近一半。根據Counterpoint的資料，一年前，企業級SSD僅佔NAND出貨量的26%，如今這一比例幾乎翻了一番。

【沙特調整原油出口路線】最新資料顯示，近期經由霍爾木茲海峽通行的船舶數量，已經降至近三個月以來的低位。目前不僅是霍爾木茲海峽，曼德海峽也正成為新的安全風險點。沙烏地阿拉伯正加大調整原油出口路線。克普勒的資料顯示，8月份，埃及地中海港口、西迪基里爾的原油日均出口量躍升至了大約230萬桶，較7月翻了一倍多，也遠高於過去兩年的水平。克普勒稱，其中大部分原油出口來自沙特，分析認為，這並不是沙特的短期應急之舉，同時繞開霍爾木茲海峽和曼德海峽，可能會成為沙特原油出口的新趨勢。 展望後市，高盛認為，在中東局勢逐步緩和的基準情景下，第四季度布倫特原油均價可能會在大約每桶80美元。但是，分析認為油價風險仍然偏向上行。（央視財經）

### 2026-08-13 17:10:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Croatia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Croatia Producer Inflation Eases to 4-Month Low — Producer prices in Croatia increased by 4.9% year-on-year in July 2026, easing from 5.7% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading in four months. The slowdown was mainly driven by a softer increase in mining and quarrying prices (13.9% vs. 18.5% in June), largely reflecting a slower rise in crude petroleum and natural gas extraction (19.5% vs. 30.8%). Manufacturing producer price inflation also moderated (4.3% vs. 5.3%), due to a sharp easing in prices of coke and refined petroleum products (38.7% vs. 54.6%). In contrast, inflation accelerated slightly for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (5.4% vs. 4.7%), while price growth also edged up for water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation activities (2.0% vs. 1.8%). On a monthly basis, producer prices declined by 0.2%, easing from a 0.3% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-08-13 17:09:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 2

Euro Area Industrial Output Stalls in June — Industrial production in the Euro Area was unchanged in June 2026, after a 0.3% rise in May and compared with market expectations of a 0.1% decrease. Output rebounded for durable consumer goods (0.3% vs -1.3% in May) and continued to increase for non-durable consumer (3% vs 3.3%) and energy (1.5% vs 2.6%). On the other hand, production fell for intermediate goods (-0.8% vs -0.2%) and capital goods (-1.4% vs 0.5%). Among the bloc’s largest economies, industrial production rose 0.2% in Germany, was unchanged in France and declined 0.7% in Spain. Year-over-year, industrial production increased 0.1%, after a 0.1% decrease in June.

【杭州公安偵破全國首起“護劇”敲詐勒索案】杭州市公安局通報，近日成功偵破全國首起以“護劇”為名實施網路輿情敲詐勒索案件，打掉了依託自媒體賬號操控輿論、脅迫影視劇組及藝人付費消災的職業化犯罪團伙。2026年5月2日，杭州公安網安民警在日常網路巡查中發現異常線索，多名影視類自媒體賬號在影視劇上線宣發節點集中釋出負面內容，待輿情發酵後又快速刪除，固定化的操作模式引起警方警覺。經專案組深入偵查，以李某、廖某為首的犯罪團伙逐漸浮出水面，該團伙運營一批粉絲總量達2020餘萬的微博賬號，專門瞄準新劇開播、綜藝宣發關鍵時段，捏造散佈不實負面資訊，多賬號聯動炒作話題、煽動粉絲對立製造輿論壓力。待影視公司、藝人經紀團隊主動溝通後，嫌疑人並不直接提“刪帖”，而是以“提供宣傳服務”“輿情最佳化合作”的名義進行談判，將刪帖包裝成“護劇”商業宣傳行為，開口索要高額費用，並透過加大發帖頻率、擴大傳播範圍的方式，形成持續輿論施壓，直至對方“花錢消災”平息風波。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-13 17:03:57  #宏觀

【李強簽署國務院令 公佈《國務院關於修改和廢止部分行政法規的決定》】國務院總理李強日前簽署國務院令，公佈《國務院關於修改和廢止部分行政法規的決定》，自2026年8月15日起施行。為全面有效貫徹實施《中華人民共和國生態環境法典》，確保生態環境法律制度體系邏輯統一、規則協調，國務院對涉及的行政法規進行了清理，決定對12部行政法規的部分條款予以修改，對3部行政法規予以廢止。修改《全國汙染源普查條例》、《生態環境監測條例》等行政法規，相關行政法規中引用的法律將於今年8月15日生態環境法典施行之日廢止的，相應將引用的法律名稱統一修改為生態環境法典；相關行政法規關於法律責任等具體事項的規定與生態環境法典不一致的，依照生態環境法典的規定作相應修改。廢止《中華人民共和國防治陸源汙染物汙染損害海洋環境管理條例》等行政法規，更好適應高質量發展和生態文明建設的新要求。 (新華社)

### 2026-08-13 17:01:47  #宏觀

【整治食品新增劑濫用 2.3萬餘件超範圍、超限量使用案件被查處】自去年4月國務院食安辦等六部門聯合部署開展食品新增劑濫用問題綜合治理以來，各地市場監管部門聚焦重點食品品類和關鍵生產加工環節，整治食品新增劑濫用違法行為，綜合治理工作取得顯著成效。各地市場監管部門以乳製品、肉製品、水產製品、澱粉及澱粉製品、食用植物油、飲料、複合調味料為重點，聚焦食品生產和餐飲加工過程中食品新增劑超範圍、超限量使用突出問題，加大監督抽檢力度，嚴厲查處防腐劑、甜味劑、著色劑濫用等突出違法行為。全國共查辦超範圍、超限量使用食品新增劑案件23603件，涉案總金額4362萬元，罰沒金額1.45億元，其中278件移送公安機關依法追究刑事責任，對食品新增劑濫用等違法行為形成強大震懾。下一步，市場監管部門將結合監管中發現的突出問題，持續加大監督執法力度，聚焦奶茶、肉類熟食、糕點、麵食、酒類等重點品種深入推進綜合治理，持續推動企業落實食品安全主體責任，規範食品新增劑使用行為，依法查處重點案件、曝光典型案例，確保綜合治理取得實效。

Namibia Inflation Rate Stable at Near 2-Year High of 4.4% — The annual inflation rate in Namibia stood at 4.4% in July 2026, the highest level since August 2024, unchanged from June. Transportation was the main source of price pressure, rising 9.3%, followed by hotels, cafés and restaurants (6.2%) and recreation and culture (5.6%), while housing & utilities (4.8%) and health (4.8%) also recorded steep increases. On a monthly basis, the CPI edged up by 0.1%, after a 0.3% increase in the month before.

### 2026-08-13 16:52:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Oman #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Oman Inflation Accelerates in July — Oman's annual inflation rose to 3.2% in July 2026, up from 2.8% in the prior month. Food inflation rose to 7.3% from 6.1%, while transport inflation increased to 6.5% from 5.5%. Prices also rose faster for recreation and culture (0.4% vs 0.3%). Meanwhile, inflation remained elevated for furnishings (3.1%) and education (2.2%), while housing and utilities costs continued to decline, falling 0.6%. In contrast, inflation eased for restaurants and hotels (3.6% vs 4.6%) and health (1.7% vs 1.8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.7%, rebounding from a 0.6% decline in June.

【我國核電總裝機規模居世界第一 中國核電交出亮眼綠色答卷】今年，我國加大核電專案建設力度，57臺機組核准在建，在運和核準在建總裝機達1.35億千瓦。核電工程建設規模穩居世界第一。作為推動經濟社會綠色轉型的重要能源，我國核電以在全國電力裝機中不到2%的佔比，貢獻了近5%的發電量。廣東、福建、遼寧、海南等4個省份的核電發電量佔比均超過15%。核電已成為東部沿海地區重要的清潔能源供給來源。7月底，我國又新核准8臺核電機組，這也是“十五五”時期首批核准的核電專案。“十五五”期間，我國核電建設繼續保持“積極安全有序發展”的原則，穩步推進東部沿海核電帶建設，因地制宜推進核能綜合利用，穩步提升核電在能源電力消費中的比重。（央視財經）

【India doubles installed capacity to shore supply; transmission lag constrains delivery】India's peak power demand topped 270 GW for the first time, roughly 70% higher than a decade ago. Installed capacity has nearly doubled over 10 years to about 552 GW, with non‑fossil sources now exceeding 300 GW. The government plans roughly $109bn of transmission investment through 2032, but grid build‑out is slower and more complex than solar plant construction: transmission corridors require land, rights‑of‑way, towers, transformers and multiple approvals, and incomplete towers can stall entire lines. Generation growth has kept pace with demand growth, but delivery constraints heighten near‑term demand for transmission, storage and financially sustainable distribution to make power available where and when needed.

US Producer Prices Seen Rising 0.2% — US producer prices likely increased by 0.2% month-over-month in July 2026, following a 0.3% fall in June. Core producer prices, which exclude the more volatile food and energy components, are forecast to rise by 0.3% in July, accelerating from the 0.2% increase recorded in June. On an annual basis, headline producer inflation is projected to slow to 4.9%, down from 5.5% in June and marking its highest level in four months. Meanwhile, annual core producer inflation is expected to decrease to 4.2% from 4.7%, also reaching its lowest reading since March.

### 2026-08-13 16:14:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Palestine #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Palestine CPI Slides 43% in July — Consumer prices in Palestine fell 43.19% year-on-year in July 2026, deepening from a 40.04% decline in June and marking another sharp drop in consumer prices. The continued year-on-year decline largely reflects the normalization of prices in Gaza following the exceptionally high price levels recorded previously. Prices in the Gaza Strip plunged 66.27%, while prices increased 1.90% in Jerusalem J1 and 1.67% in the West Bank. On a monthly basis, the CPI fell 1.68% in July, compared with a 1.6% decline in June. The monthly drop was driven by lower prices for eggs (-8.20%), fresh vegetables (-5.19%), petrol (-4.11%), diesel (-3.12%), fresh fruit (-2.41%), gas (-2.10%), fresh chicken (-1.61%), and vegetable oils (-1.40%).

### 2026-08-13 16:06:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Norway #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Norges Bank Keeps Interest Rate at 4.25% — The Norges Bank kept its policy rate steady at 4.25% in August, in line with market expectations, as inflation slowed more than anticipated over the summer. CPI inflation fell to 3% in July, while underlying inflation declined to 2.7%. However, both measures remain above the central bank’s 2% target, meaning policymakers believe it is too early to conclude that inflation is sustainably moving lower. The Bank therefore maintained its restrictive monetary policy stance and warned that another rate hike could still be necessary. Economic activity and labour-market conditions have developed broadly as expected, although housing prices fell sharply in July and construction remains weak. The krone has recovered somewhat after weakening earlier in the summer. The next major signal will come in September, when new economic forecasts are published and policymakers reassess the future path of interest rates.

### 2026-08-13 16:04:25  #公司

【劉文祥麻辣燙回應“店員徒手抓取食材”：已要求門店停業整改】8月12日，有網友發影片反映，廣州天河區沙河頂地鐵站附近的一家劉文祥麻辣燙門店，店員在製作外賣訂單時，直接徒手抓取食材。影片顯示，一位店員一手拿著食材桶和外賣訂單，另一手直接抓取製作外賣的食材，全程未佩戴工作帽和手套。8月13日，記者嘗試與影片釋出者取得聯絡，但截至發稿時，對方暫未回覆。同日，記者查詢並致電劉文祥麻辣燙沙河頂門店，經門店店長核實，影片中的門店確實是他們店。店長表示，按照規定，所有員工上崗必須佩戴工作帽，拿取食材時需佩戴手套。店長稱，影片中的店員是門店近期新入職的員工，可能由於訂單較多，忙起來之後忘記佩戴手套。“按規定必須要戴手套的，我們在忙的時候，他就有可能忘戴了。”同時，店長表示，門店此前就已決定要辭退該員工，後續也會加強對員工的培訓，避免此類事情發生。隨後，該店長告訴記者，公司已關注相關情況，要求門店在24小時內完成整改。“公司已經說了，24小時之內整改，停業24小時。”（湖北經視）

FTSE MIB Hovers at Record Levels — The FTSE MIB rose to around 53,900 on Thursday, remaining close to record levels, as stabilizing energy prices supported investor sentiment and eased some concerns around inflation expectations. Heavyweight financials were the top performers, led by UniCredit, which rose over 1% after reports showed that the ECB is leaning toward approving the takeover of Commerzbank. Its peers also started higher, including Intesa Sanpaolo (+0.8%), Unipol Gruppo (+0.9%), Banca Mediolanum (+1.1%), and Banca Monte dei Paschi di Siena (+1%). Saipem also rose 0.9% after announcing a new offshore EPCI contract in the Middle East, valued at approximately $1.8 billion. Other notable gainers were defense stocks Leonardo, Fincantieri, and Avio, which added 0.6%, 1.6%, and 1.7%, respectively.

### 2026-08-13 15:53:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Brent Halts 6-Day Rally — Brent crude fell below $88 a barrel on Thursday, ending a six-session rally as investors shifted their focus toward weakening demand prospects and continued disruption around the Strait of Hormuz. The International Energy Agency lowered its global oil demand outlook, warning that the prolonged Middle East conflict and higher prices are increasingly weighing on consumption. The agency estimates the global oil market could face a supply shortfall of 1.8 million barrels a day this quarter, more than twice its previous forecast, while supply remained 6.3 million barrels a day below year-earlier levels in July. Meanwhile, US crude inventories surged by 17.4 million barrels last week, their largest weekly increase since January 2023. Diplomatic efforts between the US and Iran to end the conflict and reopen Hormuz have made little progress, with attacks on shipping continuing and rhetoric intensifying. Despite Thursday’s decline, Brent remained nearly 5% higher for the week.

French Stocks Hover Near Flat — The CAC 40 hovered near the flatline at around 8,687 on Thursday, following the previous session's decline as investors remained cautious amid conflicting signals over efforts to secure a lasting ceasefire between the US and Iran. While reports indicated that Washington and Tehran had agreed to extend a 60-day ceasefire brokered by Pakistan, tensions remained elevated after Iran threatened to prolong the conflict until the end of President Donald Trump's term and again rejected his claims that the US has "total control" over the Strait of Hormuz. Offsetting some geopolitical concerns, softer-than-expected US inflation data reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep interest rates unchanged at its September meeting. Among the index heavyweights, gains were led by LVMH (0.5%), L'Oréal (0.4%), TotalEnergies (0.5%), and Hermès (0.9%). In contrast, STMicroelectronics (-1%), Dassault Systèmes (-0.9%), and Air Liquide (-0.4%) were among the notable decliners.

### 2026-08-13 15:41:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Poland Inflation Confirmed at 3.0% in July — The annual inflation rate in Poland accelerated to 3.0% year-on-year in July 2026 from 2.5% in the previous month, confirming preliminary estimates. The pickup was mainly driven by faster price growth for transport (7.0% vs 1.3% in June), reflecting a sharp rebound in transport costs, and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.9% vs 6.5%). Inflation also accelerated for information and communication (5.2% vs 4.0%) and recreation, sport and culture (5.4% vs 5.2%). In contrast, prices continued to decline for clothing and footwear (-3.6% vs -3.1%), while inflation eased for housing and utilities (4.5% vs 4.9%), health (4.9% vs 5.0%), education services (5.3% vs 6.0%), and restaurants and accommodation services (4.2% vs 4.5%). Food prices remained in deflation (-0.4% vs -0.2%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.8%, rebounding from a 0.5% decline in June.

UK Natural Gas Dips — UK natural gas prices fell below 148 pence a therm on Thursday but remained more than 8% higher so far this week, as supply concerns persisted amid uncertainty over efforts to resolve the Middle East conflict and reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Talks between the US and Iran appear to have hit an impasse, with both sides taking a firmer stance and President Trump insisting that Washington has full control of the key waterway and intends to keep it that way. Shipping disruptions have sharply curtailed LNG flows from the Gulf, significantly delaying cargoes from major exporter Qatar and making it difficult for Europe to rebuild gas inventories ahead of winter. Analysts anticipate European gas prices will maintain a firm floor until storage levels show clearer signs of building before the heating season begins. At the same time, intense summer heatwaves across Southern and Central Europe have ramped up gas-fired power generation to meet cooling needs, diverting supplies from storage.

### 2026-08-13 15:38:01  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #000660.KS #Impact bullish #005930.KS

【AI surge pushes KOSPI into technical bull market; durability questioned】South Korea’s KOSPI rose 3.21% on Thursday, completing a more-than-20% rebound from the July 30 close low and entering a technical bull market; SK Hynix and Samsung Electronics gained about 6% and 4% respectively. The index had plunged 22% in July, its worst monthly drop since the global financial crisis. KB Securities global investment strategist Peter Kim said the AI-led rally and earnings upside were present through the selloff, arguing fundamentals—not valuations—are driving the market rebound; he cited technical factors and flows as the root of the prior selloff and said regulator tightening and brokers’ resumption of normal risk controls have eased forced deleveraging. Phillip Wool, head of research at Ruilian Caizhi, said the market is effectively trading as an AI-hardware market: the rebound reflects receding forced selling, dip-buying and FOMO, and has been supported by stronger-than-expected results that lift AI spend and hardware earnings forecasts; he warned a setback—such as weak capex guidance or renewed Fed tightening—could prompt a pullback. Global X strategist Billy Leung said corporate-governance and value-unlocking plans should help narrow Korea’s valuation discount, and he leans toward a fundamentals-led rally given rising semiconductor profit forecasts, but rising retail participation and index concentration show early late-cycle signals. Fibonacci Asset Management CEO Zheng Renyun urged caution: his base case is a broader bull trend supported by semiconductor earnings and improved risk appetite, but he expects the pace to slow and for consolidation after sharp gains.

### 2026-08-13 15:37:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Hungary Industrial Output Hits Over 3-Year High — Industrial production in Hungary soared 10.1% year-on-year in June 2026, confirming preliminary estimates and following a 0.4% decline in the previous month. This marked the strongest growth since September 2022, as manufacturing output, which accounts for 96% of total industrial production, climbed 10.8% in June after stagnating in May. Production rebounded in transport equipment (21.0% vs -0.5% in May), electrical equipment (1.8% vs -2.6%), rubber and plastic products and other non-metallic mineral products (2.8% vs -3.2%), and basic metals and fabricated metal products, except machinery and equipment (8.1% vs -3.0%), while output of computer, electronic and optical products rose faster (49.7% vs 42.1%). Additionally, production accelerated in mining and quarrying (60.8% vs 43.7%), while electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply rebounded (0.3% vs -9.9%). On a seasonally and working-day adjusted basis, industrial output fell 1.4% in June, reversing a 2.7% increase in May.

【著手AI成本可預見性 Compute Exchange推出詞元遠期合約】海量資金即將湧入晶片與資料中心賽道，各家企業持續大手筆投入AI技術自研，幾乎所有相關參與方都在想方設法，提升自身投入與收益的可預測性。據報道，初創算力交易平臺Compute Exchange原本業務是撮合GPU持有方與短期租用者，現在拓展同類撮合業務，推出詞元遠期合約（Token forwards）。這屬於場外私下協商合約，允許企業最長鎖定未來六個月的詞元採購價格，對沖後續AI使用成本波動風險。 詞元遠期合約的定價模式和GPU合約存在區別：GPU合約一般鎖定執行AI模型所需算力的小時單價；詞元合約則在更長週期內鎖定統一價格，交易標的也是企業日常業務真正消耗的資源。 客戶可選擇六家簽約推理服務商提供的多款主流開源權重模型。遠期合約和CME籌備的標準化場內期貨並不完全等同，但標誌著金融市場套保機制，進一步延伸到更多型別AI成本專案。

### 2026-08-13 15:29:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Slovakia Inflation Eases to 19-Month Low — Slovakia's annual inflation rate eased to 3.3% in July 2026 from 3.5% in June, remaining above market expectations of 3%. It was the lowest reading since December 2024, largely due to a deeper decline in food and non-alcoholic beverage prices (-2.1% vs -0.5%). Inflation also slowed for alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (1.8% vs 3.1%), insurance and financial services (4.6% vs 4.9%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.6% vs 3.2%). Meanwhile, price growth accelerated for housing and utilities (7.4% vs 7.3%), transport (7.0% vs 5.3%), clothing and footwear (0.8% vs 0.6%), health (5.1% vs 4.9%), information and communication (2.2% vs 2.0%), and recreation, sport and culture (5.2% vs 4.8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.1%, compared with market expectations and the previous month's 0.1% decline. Core inflation, which excludes regulated prices, edged down to 1.7% from 1.9% in June, marking its lowest level since March 2021.

### 2026-08-13 15:29:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Holds Near 4-Week Low — The yields on India’s 10-year G-Sec weakened to around 6.76%, hovering near four-week lows as inflation data from India and the US reinforced expectations that neither central bank is likely to raise rates soon. Market participants assessed India’s July CPI, which rose to 4.45% from 4.38% in June, below the 4.50% forecast and comfortably within the RBI’s tolerance range. Meanwhile, US annual inflation slowed to 3.4% in July from 3.5% in June, marginally reducing expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike next month and supporting US Treasuries. However, the decline in Indian yields remained limited as Brent crude held around $88 per barrel, keeping inflation risks in focus for the world’s third-largest oil importer. The US 10-year Treasury yield also remained elevated, while market participants shifted their attention to Friday’s INR 320 billion government bond auction, which is expected to provide the next major direction for yields.

Iron Ore Falls on Demand Worries — Iron ore futures declined toward CNY 700 per ton, approaching 14-month lows as weak profitability among Chinese steelmakers continues to constrain the potential for a meaningful recovery in ore demand. Recent data also showed China imported 108.086 million tons of iron ore and concentrates in July, down 4.09% from June but still 3.5% higher than a year earlier. Meanwhile, slower consumer and producer inflation in China last month pointed to persistent weakness in domestic demand. On the supply side, however, tightening shipments continued to offer some support to prices. Industry data showed global iron ore shipments fell by 1.38 million tons in the week through August 9 to around 32 million tons, while volumes arriving at Chinese ports dropped by 13.1 million tons to 18.9 million tons.

### 2026-08-13 15:18:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Spain July Inflation Rate Revised Slightly Up to 3.6% — Spain's annual inflation rate was revised slightly higher to 3.6% in July 2026 from a preliminary estimate of 3.5% and June's reading of 3.2%. This marked the highest rate since May 2024, mainly driven by fuel and lubricant prices for personal vehicles, which increased more than in July 2025. The core inflation rate, excluding unprocessed food and energy, also rose to 3.0% from 2.9% in June, in line with estimates and matching May’s near two-year peak. The EU-harmonized annual rate hit 3.9%, its highest since February 2023, up from 3.6% in June and above the preliminary estimate of 3.8%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.3%, up from the earlier estimate of 0.2%, but easing from the 0.6% increase recorded in June.

### 2026-08-13 15:12:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Current Account #Importance 1

Lithuania Current Account Surplus Widens in June — Lithuania’s current account surplus widened sharply to EUR 437.5 million in June 2026 from EUR 191.5 million in the same month a year earlier. The services account surplus increased to EUR 985.4 million from EUR 791.9 million, while the secondary income account shifted to a EUR 346.5 million surplus from a deficit of EUR 17.9 million. On the other hand, the goods account deficit widened to EUR 695.1 million from EUR 408.5 million, driven largely by a larger increase in imports, which rose by EUR 824.7 million to EUR 4,175.1 million, compared with a EUR 538.1 million increase in exports to EUR 3,480 million. The primary income deficit also rose to EUR 199.4 million from EUR 174 million a year ago.

### 2026-08-13 15:12:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Current Account #Importance 1

Turkey Current Account Deficit Widens in June — Turkey's current account deficit widened to $4.19 billion in June 2026 from $2.27 billion in the same month a year earlier, but came in below the expected $5.0 billion shortfall. The goods deficit increased to $8.53 billion from $6.52 billion, while the services surplus rose to $6.85 billion from $6.09 billion. The primary income deficit widened to $1.79 billion from $1.76 billion, while the secondary income deficit increased to $0.73 billion from $0.08 billion. Excluding gold and energy, the current account recorded a $1.46 billion surplus. In the first half of the year, the cumulative current account deficit reached $34.55 billion. On an annualized basis, the current account deficit reached $38.9 billion in June, with the goods deficit at $76.5 billion and the services surplus at $63.7 billion.

【China issues 15th Five-Year plan for safety and emergency equipment industry】The Ministry of Industry and Information Technology and the Ministry of Emergency Management jointly issued a 15th Five-Year development plan for the safety and emergency equipment industry, targeting marked improvements in scale, technology, product quality and application breadth by 2030. The plan aims for key-area industry scale to reach 1.4 tln yuan by 2030, to secure breakthroughs in core technologies and deliver internationally leading flagship products, and to establish 10 competitive industrial clusters and 70 industry-specific parks. It directs wider deployment of high-tech emergency equipment — including increased household and community provisioning — and seeks to cultivate internationally competitive leading firms, specialized "small giant" enterprises and single-product manufacturing champions. The plan details 15 concrete tasks across four pillars: industry innovation, equipment application promotion, scaling industry players, and optimizing the industrial ecosystem.

### 2026-08-13 15:08:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Aluminum Extends Decline — Aluminum futures in the UK fell to around $3,270 per tonne, retreating further from a seven-week high, after Emirates Global Aluminium reaffirmed its timeline to restore full production at the Al Taweelah smelter by Q1 next year, easing concerns over supply from the Gulf. The company was forced to cut output after the smelter was hit by Iranian missiles and drones during the early stages of the war. The restoration is expected to improve supply prospects from the Middle East, which accounted for around 10% of global aluminum production prior to the conflict. Prices had jumped recently amid lower feedstock production at Norsk Hydro’s plant in Brazil, adding to concerns over Gulf supply as uncertainty persisted over a deal that could reopen the Strait of Hormuz, a key export route for regional smelters. Further easing supply fears, the owners of Australia’s largest aluminum smelter secured a $1.8 billion government bailout, allowing operations to continue.

The broader index hit its highest level since July 6, as traders maintained the upward momentum in the technology sector. Electronic technology stocks advanced 1.5%, following a 1.1% rise on Wednesday. TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, advanced 0.8%. MediaTek and Delta Electronics surged 5.4% and 4.8%, respectively. Meanwhile, Yageo Corporation, a world leader in electronic passive components, soared 10% after the company became the fifth-largest holding of Norway's sovereign wealth fund. A rally on Wall Street overnight also lifted sentiment, as moderated inflation eased expectations of a Fed rate hike at its upcoming meeting.

### 2026-08-13 15:02:44  #國際

【韓政府公佈新增首都圈住房供應方案 含綠帶用地】韓國國土交通部13日在政府首爾辦公大樓釋出了“為穩定租房及房屋交易市場的住宅迅速供應方案”。此次方案是去年9月7日政府公佈的計劃在2030年前在首都圈供應135萬套住房的樓市新規跟進措施，旨在進一步確保住房供應的同時，縮短政策公佈與實際供應到位之間的時間差距。政府計劃透過此次方案在首都圈額外供應超過23萬套住房，包括在開發限制區（綠帶）內規劃的10萬套新建住宅用地。為加快公共用地實際投入供應的開發速度，被指定為新建住房用地後至開工的時期將從原先的68個月縮短至37個月。首都圈八成多住房供應依託民間專案，因此政府還計劃幫助民間改造專案加快速度，以加快住房供應程序。

ASX 200 Down 0.2% at Close — The ASX 200 fell 21 points, or 0.2%, to end at 9,188 on Thursday, marking a second day of weakness as communications, logistics, and manufacturing dragged. Restrictive monetary policy kept sentiment cautious, with RBA’s Christopher Kent stressing tight settings are needed to rein in inflation. In top trading partner China, policymakers signaled no broad easing and warning that history shows aggressive stimulus often precedes sharper shocks, adding to the bearish mood. Insurance Australia Group slumped 5.1% on weaker cash earnings, hit by lower investment income and rising claims from severe weather. Commonwealth Bank lost 2.2%, though ANZ rose 4.5% after a modest profit lift. Miners were broadly lower, with Rio Tinto down 3.6% and South32 off 0.6%. In contrast, ASX Ltd surged 9% after reporting a more than 5% rise in annual underlying profit, buoyed by heightened global volatility that boosted trading and post-trade activity.

### 2026-08-13 15:01:38  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Apple #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #AAPL.O #Impact bullish #FDX.O #NKE.O

【Tariff refunds hit corporate books, temporarily lifting U.S. profits】After the U.S. Supreme Court ruled portions of tariffs imposed under the International Emergency Economic Powers Act lacked legal authority, refunds have begun appearing in corporate filings. More than 40 S&P 500 companies have disclosed roughly $9.6bn in refunds, led by Apple (~$2.2bn), Nike (~$986m) and FedEx (~$800m). Apple reported EPS of $2.02 for the quarter, with refunds adding $0.11 and boosting gross margin by about 2 percentage points. These payments are reversals of past tariff costs, not signs of stronger demand or operating improvement, and should not be extrapolated as recurring profit gains. The impact will vary by firm: FedEx says it largely paid duties on behalf of customers and plans to return about $800m to shippers and consumers, while some retailers may retain refunds or use them to cut prices. In the near term refunds support cash flow and margins for import-heavy firms, but they should be stripped out when assessing underlying earnings trends.

【Mecca joint defence pact raises Middle East order uncertainty — nascent alliance or strategic hedge?】Saudi Arabia, Turkey and Pakistan have signed a Mecca joint defence pact that could shift regional power dynamics by enhancing members' autonomous deterrent capacity and easing some US security burdens, though its operational credibility remains uncertain. A Foundation for Defense of Democracies expert says the agreement appears more focused on economic and commercial cooperation than on a NATO‑style security alliance; its deterrent value depends on joint planning, command arrangements and crisis procedures. A Pakistani policy analyst at CUNY warned the pact should not be read as an Article‑5 equivalent for now. Even a limited military arrangement may harden alignments—prompting counter‑cooperation from countries such as Israel and Greece—and therefore raise geopolitical tensions in the region.

### 2026-08-13 14:47:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Construction Output #Importance 1

Hungary Construction Output Decline Eases in June — Hungary’s construction output fell by 1.2% year-on-year in June 2026, easing sharply from a 10.7% decline in the previous month. The decline was driven by a 5.5% contraction in building construction from 4.6% increase in May. Meanwhile output in other construction increased 5.6% from slumping 14.9% in the previous month, supported by a 28.3% rise in civil engineering, mainly due to a 49.6% expansion in road and railway construction. Meanwhile, production in specialised construction, the industry’s largest segment, rose 0.6% from a year earlier. On a seasonally and working-day adjusted monthly basis, construction activity fell by 3.9% in June, following the previous month’s increase. In the first half of 2026, total construction output declined by 0.7% compared with the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-08-13 14:47:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Swiss Producer and Import Prices Fall Again in July — Switzerland’s producer and import prices dropped by 2.1% year-on-year in July 2026, the same pace as in the previous month and extending a more than three-year deflationary streak. The latest figure also remained the sharpest annual decline since March, reflecting continued weakness in both domestic and imported prices. Producer prices continued to decline (-2.3% vs -2.7% in June), driven by lower prices mainly for agricultural and forestry products (-5.8%), electricity and gas supply (-6.9%), and water treatment and distribution (-4%). Import prices also declined further (-1.5% vs -0.8%), as costs dropped for mining and quarrying products (-19.5%) and manufacturing products (-0.9%), which more than offset an increase in agricultural products (1.7%). On a monthly basis, producer and import prices fell by 0.1% in July, following a 0.3% decrease in June and marking a third consecutive monthly drop, mainly driven by lower petroleum-related prices.

UK Business Investment Growth Accelerates — Business investment in the United Kingdom rose by 1.7% quarter-on-quarter in the three months to June 2026, exceeding market expectations of a 0.5% decline and accelerating from 0.9% in the previous period, according to preliminary estimates. This marked the strongest growth since the first quarter of 2025, highlighting a continued recovery in corporate spending. The increase was largely driven by a significant rise in spending on ICT equipment and other machinery and equipment, while investment declined mainly in intellectual property products. On an annual basis, business investment grew 0.8% in the second quarter, rebounding from a 1.3% decline in the fourth quarter. Meanwhile, overall gross fixed capital formation increased by 0.4% quarter-on-quarter and 1.6% year-on-year.

Silver Eases Ahead of US PPI Data — Silver slipped below $65 an ounce on Thursday after reaching a seven-week high earlier in the session, as investors adopted a cautious stance ahead of US producer inflation data for further insights on recent price trends. Data released Wednesday showed US consumer inflation eased for a second consecutive month to 3.4% in July, while increasing just 0.1% from the previous month. Markets now see around a 40% chance of a 25 basis point rate hike from the Federal Reserve in September, down from nearly 50% a day earlier. Precious metals also remained under pressure from elevated oil prices as hopes for a deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz stayed limited. Escalating rhetoric between the US and Iran amid stalled negotiations further reduced expectations for an imminent agreement.
